## Wave 2 idempotency key retention, ticket #42 follow-up

The owner's decisions of 2026-10-07 on #42: a configurable retention for idempotency keys with asynchronous, batched cleanup outside the request path, and the context's identity rules in [ADR 0007](adr/0007-operation-context-set-by-the-scope-opener.md#amendment-2026-10-07-ticket-42-follow-up-the-context-in-the-idempotency-key). Each test starts a Host of the application whose `TimeProvider` is the test clock, so the store stamps a key with the test's time and the cleanup ticks when the test advances the clock; `CountNoteCommand` answers a number no other run answers, so a replay tells itself apart from a new run.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A key replays for its whole retention, 24 hours by default: one reserved 23 h 59 min before a cleanup replays after it, while the cleanup removes one reserved earlier, whose request id then runs as a new request. A key records the Host `TimeProvider`'s time it was reserved | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_key_replays_for_its_whole_retention_and_once_a_cleanup_removed_it_its_request_runs_as_a_new_one` | Red when the cutoff ignores the retention (the younger key is removed and runs again), and when the store stamps the system clock (no key expires on the test clock). |
| One cleanup removes more expired keys than a batch holds, `BatchSize` keys per statement until a batch is short | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_cleanup_removes_more_expired_keys_than_one_batch_in_batches_of_the_configured_size` | Five keys with `BatchSize` 2: three statements, as `IdempotencyKeysRemoved` logs. Red with one batch per tick, and without the `limit`. |
| A failed cleanup is logged and the next one removes the expired keys; the Host keeps running | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_failed_cleanup_is_logged_and_the_next_one_removes_the_expired_keys` | The `reserved_at` column is renamed for one tick. Red when the failure escapes the tick, which stops the Host. |
| `Retention = Timeout.InfiniteTimeSpan` keeps every key, and no cleanup runs | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_host_whose_retention_is_infinite_keeps_every_key` | Thirty days on the test clock, then a second of real time. Red when the cleanup runs anyway. |
| A Host with a retention, interval or batch size out of range fails to start, naming each: a retention that is not positive or exceeds 36500 days, an interval outside `PeriodicTimer`'s 1 ms to `uint.MaxValue - 1` ms, a batch size under 1 | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_host_whose_retention_interval_or_batch_size_is_out_of_range_fails_to_start_naming_each` (zero, -1 h and 0; 36501 days and 0.1 ms; a 50-day interval) | Review fix F2. Red without the options check, and without each bound: the 50-day interval then fails the start with `PeriodicTimer`'s own exception. |
| A repeat whose expired key a cleanup removes after its insert found the key held, and before it read the recorded success, runs as a new request and leaves one key | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_repeat_whose_expired_key_a_cleanup_removes_between_its_reservation_and_its_read_runs_as_a_new_request` | Review fix F3. An EF Core command interceptor on the Stub's DbContext advances the test clock right after that insert and waits until the cleanup removed the key. Red with one reservation attempt: the repeat throws. |
| A key table created before `CreateIdempotencyTable` added `reserved_at` fails the Operation with an `InvalidOperationException` that names the column and the SQL that adds it, and commits nothing | `IdempotencyRetentionTests.A_key_table_without_reserved_at_fails_the_operation_naming_the_column_and_its_fix` | Review fix F1: the column is renamed for the test. Red without the mapping of SQLSTATE 42703. No forward migration: the earlier shape never shipped, so only a local database migrated from an earlier Wave 2 build has it. |
| #42's guarantees hold with the timestamp and the shared null-tenant sentinel | `IdempotencyTests`; `PouryaKit.Cqrs.Tests.IdempotencyTests` | `IdempotencyTable.NoTenant` is now an alias of `OperationContext.NoTenant`. |
| `PouryaKit.Persistence.EFCore` references `Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting.Abstractions` | `PouryaKit.ArchitectureTests.PackageGraphTests` | Its section C row, under the standing rule for MIT `Microsoft.Extensions.*` packages. |
| The packed API compiles in a clean consumer | `scripts/packaging/consumers/PouryaKit.Persistence.EFCore.cs` | Sets every `PouryaKitIdempotencyOptions` property. |
| Packages | No package added or bumped | `PouryaKit.Persistence.EFCore` gains `PouryaKitIdempotencyOptions` and the direct reference `Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting.Abstractions` 10.0.12 (MIT), the version `Directory.Packages.props` already pins; `CreateIdempotencyTable` adds the `reserved_at` column and its index, so the sample's and the Stub's `IdempotencyKeys` migrations create them. |

`python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the follow-up's source (commit `bdf8c7e`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 20 packages, 22 clean consumers, negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.68`, with the Release runtime suite at 691 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. The package-only consumer of Persistence.EFCore built `PouryaKitIdempotencyOptions` from `consumers/PouryaKit.Persistence.EFCore.cs` against the packed packages only, and the `modular-monolith` composition built the sample, whose `IdempotencyKeys` migration now creates `reserved_at`, from packages.
After the review fix round (findings F1 to F3), `python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the fixed source (commit `9fdee47`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 20 packages, 22 clean consumers, negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.71`, with the Release runtime suite at 695 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped.

## Wave 2 cross-process tracing and standard Host recipes, ticket #52

Spec #33, its observability, outgoing-retry and template decisions, and [ADR 0009](adr/0009-w3c-trace-context-through-the-outbox.md)'s #52 amendment. The broker path was probed first, at `8b7ab96` before any change: `parent-id` and `pouryakit-tracestate` crossed RabbitMQ, and the receiving Host's fan-out copies named no parent unless Wolverine's source was sampled, so Inventory's delivery started a new trace (the amendment's table). `CrossProcessTracingTests` runs `ModularMonolith.Host` with a broker and `Inventory.Host`, each on its own database and joined only by RabbitMQ in Testcontainers, both in the test process, because an `ActivityListener` sees its own process only. The recipes are template content in `samples/ModularMonolith/src/ModularMonolith.ServiceDefaults`, not a Kit package; `Inventory.Host` composes them. No telemetry backend: listeners, and an exporter the test adds.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A product created in the Catalog Host and stocked by `Inventory.Host` over RabbitMQ is one trace: Inventory's delivery Operation has the upstream trace id and tracestate, and its parent is the publishing Operation in the Catalog Host, or, with `Wolverine` sampled, Wolverine's span of the copy, whose parent is Wolverine's span of the broker envelope, whose parent is the publishing Operation; on the first attempt and on a stored retry | `CrossProcessTracingTests.A_product_created_in_the_catalog_host_and_stocked_by_inventory_host_is_one_trace_with_the_upstream_tracestate` (both listener configurations) | Red, with only the Kit's source listened, without the fan-out's `pouryakit-traceparent` write and without the delivery's fallback read; the Wolverine-sampled row stays green by design. Red in both without `pouryakit-tracestate` among the copied headers, and without `AddPouryaKitObservability()` in `Inventory.Host`. |
| `Inventory.Host`'s deliveries reach exporters added to the service defaults' own tracer and meter providers: the `pouryakit.operation` activity of the delivery and a `pouryakit.operation.duration` point tagged `InventoryModule`, `Deliver`, `success` | `HostRecipeTests.Inventory_hosts_deliveries_reach_the_exporters_of_its_service_defaults` | `SimpleActivityExportProcessor` and `BaseExportingMetricReader` over the test's own `BaseExporter`s, `MeterProvider.ForceFlush()` once the message store settled, since the rows commit before the Operation records its duration and ends its activity (review finding F2; with the delivery held 2 s after its commit, red waiting for the rows only, green waiting for the store). Red without the `PouryaKit` source in the recipe's tracing, and without its meter. |
| `/alive` stays `Healthy` while `/health` answers 503 for a database that stopped answering, and `Healthy` again once it answers | `HostRecipeTests.Liveness_stays_healthy_while_readiness_reports_the_database_the_host_cannot_reach` | The test's database is closed with `alter database … with allow_connections false` and `pg_terminate_backend`, then reopened. Red with `/alive` asking every check, and without the `databases` check. In a real process, with the PostgreSQL container stopped: `/alive` `Healthy (200)`, `/health` `Unhealthy (503)` (smoke below). |
| With the Serilog recipe, the Kit's logs reach a sink the Host registers | `HostRecipeTests.With_serilog_the_kits_logs_reach_a_sink_the_host_registers` | The Dispatcher's `Deliver succeeded` entry, through an `ILogEventSink` service; the fixture's `RecordedLogs` provider receives nothing once Serilog owns the logger factory. Red without `ReadFrom.Services`. |
| A client that keeps the service defaults is retried by one pipeline of the Kit's: a GET four times (three retries), a POST once | `HostRecipeTests.A_client_that_keeps_the_service_defaults_retries_a_read_through_one_pipeline_and_sends_a_write_once` (GET and POST) | WireMock.Net answers 503 with `Retry-After: 0`. Red, POST, with Aspire's `AddStandardResilienceHandler()` in the defaults; red, GET, with the Kit's handler added twice (16 attempts). |
| A client that replaces the defaults' pipeline with its own makes exactly its configured attempts (two retries, three requests), each with the Operation Context | `HostRecipeTests.A_client_that_replaces_the_service_defaults_pipeline_makes_its_configured_attempts_each_with_the_operation_context` | `RemoveAllResilienceHandlers()`, then `AddPouryaKitResilience().Configure(…)`, on a Web Host of its own with `AddServiceDefaults()`. Red without the removal: the client's pipeline stacks inside the default one. |
| An unnamed client cannot change the replay policy of the clients that keep the service defaults: `AddPouryaKitResilience(HttpReplay.AllRequests)` on the client registered with an empty name throws `ArgumentException`, and a named client that keeps the defaults still sends a POST once | `HostRecipeTests.An_unnamed_client_cannot_make_the_service_defaults_pipeline_replay_writes` | Review finding F1 of `b532fef`: the defaults' builder has no name, so their pipeline is `-standard`, the name `AddHttpClient(string.Empty)` gets too. Red without the refusal: no exception, and the named client's POST sent four times. |
| Cancelling a call on the service defaults' own pipeline stops further attempts | `HostRecipeTests.Cancelling_a_call_through_the_service_defaults_pipeline_stops_further_attempts` | The client keeps the defaults; WireMock.Net answers 503 with `Retry-After: 1`, the call is cancelled once the first attempt arrived, and one request is all WireMock.Net sees 1.5 s later. Cancellation is the resilience pipeline's own behaviour, with no Kit switch to turn off; red when the call is sent without the token. |
| `Inventory.Host` starts with the recipes as its own process and answers its health endpoints | Smoke: `postgres:18.6` and `rabbitmq:4.3.6` containers, then `DOTNET_ENVIRONMENT=Development ConnectionStrings__app=… ConnectionStrings__broker=… dotnet run --no-build --project samples/ModularMonolith/src/Inventory.Host -- --urls http://127.0.0.1:5252`, `curl http://127.0.0.1:5252/alive` and `/health` | `Healthy (200)` from both, Serilog's console sink writing the log; with the PostgreSQL container stopped, `/alive` `Healthy (200)` and `/health` `Unhealthy (503)`. `RabbitMqDeliveryTests`' child-process test runs `Program`, Serilog recipe included. |
| The standalone outgoing HTTP proof needs no ServiceDefaults; the broker tests keep their behaviour | `OutgoingHttpTests` (unchanged); `RabbitMqDeliveryTests` | `BrokerFixture` serves `Inventory.Host` in memory with `UseTestServer()`; the child process gets `ASPNETCORE_URLS=http://127.0.0.1:0`. |
| Packages | Sample-only, recorded in [`docs/provenance/ModularMonolith.md`](provenance/ModularMonolith.md) | `OpenTelemetry.Extensions.Hosting` and `.Exporter.OpenTelemetryProtocol` 1.19.1, `OpenTelemetry.Instrumentation.AspNetCore`, `.Http` and `.Runtime` 1.19.0, `Serilog.AspNetCore` 10.0.0, `Serilog.Sinks.Console` 6.1.1, `Serilog.Sinks.OpenTelemetry` 4.2.0 (all `Apache-2.0`), `Microsoft.Extensions.ServiceDiscovery` 10.10.0 (`MIT`). No Kit package references them, and no Kit package changed its references. |
| The copied Proving Sample builds `Inventory.Host` and the recipes from packages too | `scripts/packaging/validate.py`, the `modular-monolith` composition's `entries` in `inventory.json` | Both entry projects restore and build against the packed packages. |
| Out of scope | | A `PouryaKit.Aspire` package or a third template; a RabbitMQ health check (WolverineFx 6.46 registers none); the `Wolverine` source in the recipe's tracing; head sampling |

## Wave 2 monolith and modular-monolith templates, ticket #53

Spec #33, its template decisions, and plan I.1 and I.3's "Project template" row. The smallest real probe ran first: a skeleton `PouryaKit.Templates` packed with `dotnet pack`, installed into a template hive of its own, and both templates generated with `-n Acme.Shop` confirmed the `sourceName` renaming, the monolith's exclusions and a restore from the produced feed through `validate.py`'s `NuGet.Config` shape. The templates' own proofs run in the solutions they generate; `validate.py` runs them.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A product created over HTTP with an `Idempotency-Key` answers 201 with its id and versioned location, is read back and listed with a filter and a sort, and its repeat answers the same status, body and location without a second product, with a bearer token of a test issuer the Host verifies | The generated `HostTests.A_product_created_with_an_idempotency_key_is_read_and_listed_and_its_repeat_creates_nothing`, in both templates | `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` over PostgreSQL in Testcontainers, no `PouryaKit.Testing`. Red once in the generated monolith with `.RequireIdempotencyKey()` removed: the repeat answered 500. *Superseded on 2026-10-10 by ticket #87: the smoke tests host the Host `AppHost` composes through `PouryaKit.Testing`'s `HostFixture` ([its section](#pre-wave-3-test-infrastructure-and-the-template-smoke-test-ticket-87)).* |
| `/alive` answers 200 through the service defaults | The generated `HostTests.The_host_answers_alive_through_the_service_defaults`, in both | Red once with `MapDefaultEndpoints()` removed (401). The database is up, so the test does not show that liveness asks nothing outside the process: that is the recipe's own proof, `HostRecipeTests` (#52). Renamed in the fix round (review finding F3 of `f29e732`). |
| A generated Host joins a configured broker under a service name that is a valid queue name for any project name | The generated `HostTests.The_host_joins_a_configured_broker_under_its_derived_service_name`, in both; `TemplateTests.Each_generated_host_joins_a_broker_under_the_service_name_its_template_derives_the_same_way_in_both` | Review finding F1 of `f29e732`: both Hosts passed the `sourceName` as their service name, which the engine rewrites to the name as code writes it (`acme_shop` for `-n acme_shop`), a name `UsePouryaKitRabbitMq` refuses, so such a Host failed as it was composed with a broker. Both templates now pass the token of one derived `serviceName` symbol, the project's name lower-cased with each run of other characters a hyphen (`acme-shop` for `Acme.Shop`, `acme_shop` or `My Shop`). The smoke test composes the Host with `ConnectionStrings:broker` and no hosted service running, so no broker is needed, and reads the name the Kit's extension gives Wolverine. Red once in the generated solutions: the old value `Acme.Shop` (expected `acme-shop`), and `acme_shop` (`ArgumentException` as the Host is composed). The structure rule's negative case is the old line. |
| A command that declares a permission whose check is not registered stops the Host when it is built, in both compositions, through one check | The generated `HostTests.The_host_does_not_build_when_a_declared_permission_has_no_registered_check`, in both; `SecurityTests.A_command_or_query_that_declares_a_permission_whose_check_no_composition_root_registered_fails_the_host_when_built` | At #53 the check moved from Modularity's internal `HostModules` to Cqrs's public `EnsurePouryaKitDeclaredAuthorizers()`, which Modularity's factory called and the monolith's Host passed to `ConfigureContainer`; red once in the generated monolith with the call replaced by `_ => { }`: the Host built. Since #61 it is one of the composition checks of Cqrs's `AddPouryaKitCompositionChecks`, which `AddPouryaKitModules` and the monolith's Host call, run when the container is built. The message names the Composition Root that registers the handler rather than the Module, which Cqrs does not know. |
| A product Catalog creates over HTTP opens its stock item in Inventory, in the one modular Host | The generated `CrossModuleTests.A_product_catalog_creates_over_http_opens_its_stock_item_in_inventory`, modular monolith only | Red once with Inventory's `ProductCreatedV1` subscription removed from the generated copy: no stock item after 30 s. |
| The monolith runs without Modularity: nothing it generates names `PouryaKit.Modularity` or one of its types or registrations, and its restored closure holds no Modularity | `TemplateTests.Nothing_the_monolith_generates_names_modularity_and_the_modular_monolith_does`; `validate.py`, `template-monolith` with `PouryaKit.Modularity` excluded | The negative case adds a sample Gateway endpoint, a project reference and an `AddPouryaKitModules` call. |
| The same business code in both compositions, never a copy | `TemplateTests.The_template_content_holds_no_copy_of_a_proving_sample_file`; the modular smoke tests passing on content packed from the sample | `PouryaKit.Templates.csproj` packs each Module's C# files, the Host's `CallerPermissions` and `LoggedNewProductAnnouncer` and the service defaults' `Extensions.cs` from `samples/ModularMonolith/src` when it is packed. |
| Every composition-specific sample file the monolith excludes has its own file at that path, so a new Gateway endpoint in the sample fails a test, not a generated build | `TemplateTests.Every_sample_file_the_monolith_excludes_has_the_monoliths_own_file_in_its_place` | `CatalogProducts.cs` needs none: no monolith code asks `ICatalogProducts`. |
| Each template project references what the sample's project at its place does, Kit project references read as packages, but Modularity in the monolith's Catalog | `TemplateTests.Each_template_project_references_what_the_samples_project_at_its_place_does` | Billing, Catalog, `Catalog.Contracts`, Inventory and the service defaults. |
| Third-party versions are the Kit's, and every Kit package has the one version packing writes | `TemplateTests.Each_template_pins_the_kits_third_party_versions_and_one_kit_version_that_packing_writes` | An MSBuild step on `TargetsForTfmSpecificContentInPackage`, after MinVer, replaces the placeholder `0.0.0-pouryakit` in each `Directory.Packages.props` with the package version and fails the pack when the placeholder is missing. |
| The templates install, generate, restore and build from the produced packages only, and their smoke tests pass | `validate.py`, compositions `template-monolith` and `template-modular-monolith` | Installed into `template-hive` in the evidence directory, so a user's template list is untouched; `PouryaKit.Testing` is refused in both closures. *Superseded on 2026-10-10 by ticket #87: `PouryaKit.Testing` and, through it, `PouryaKit.Modularity` are allowed in the generated test project's closure, and refused in every other project's and in the Host's Release publish.* |
| Each generated Host starts as a real process | Smoke on the generated solutions: `postgres:18.6` in a container, `dotnet tool restore`, `dotnet ef database update --project src/Modules/<Module>/<Module> --startup-project src/Acme.Shop.Host --context <Module>DbContext` for each Module, then `dotnet run --project src/Acme.Shop.Host` (its `http` launch profile, Development) with `ConnectionStrings__app` and `Authentication__Schemes__Bearer__*` set | Both: `/alive` 200 `Healthy` within 3 s, `/health` 200, `GET /catalog/v2/products?size=1` 200 with an empty Page, an unauthenticated `POST /catalog/v2/categories` 401; the monolith migrated Catalog's schema, the modular monolith Catalog's, Billing's and Inventory's. |
| One resilience pipeline with the service defaults | Both generated `Program.cs` | The `downstream` client adds only `AddPouryaKitOperationContext()`; the recipe's `ConfigureHttpClientDefaults` gives it `AddPouryaKitResilience()`. *Since ticket #87 (2026-10-10) the client is registered in both generated `AppHost.cs`, which `Program.cs` runs.* |

## Wave 2 code generation for production, ticket #58

Spec #33, the owner's decision of 2026-10-07 that this is built in Wave 2, and [ADR 0004](adr/0004-commit-through-wolverine-ef-outbox.md)'s #58 amendment, following Wolverine's code generation guide for the pinned 6.46.0 (`JasperFx/wolverine` commit `dc63f46`, `docs/guide/codegen.md`) and its sample (`JasperFx/CritterStackSamples` commit `76ce8e2`, `CqrsMinimalApi/`). The early probe ran on the sample Host before the rest was built: `dotnet run -- codegen write` wrote `Internal/Generated/WolverineHandlers/GeneratedHandlerRegistry.cs` alone, an empty registry, and `dotnet run -- describe` named `PouryaKit.Messaging.Wolverine` as Wolverine's application assembly, the one Static mode loads generated types from; the Kit now sets it to the entry assembly. Built in Release without the compiler and started in Production against PostgreSQL, the sample Host then logged "Static with pre-generated types being loaded from ModularMonolith.Host" and Wolverine's missing-registry warning, built its message storage and started.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Outside Development a Kit Host whose handler chain has no generated type does not start: Wolverine throws `MissingPreBuiltTypesException`, naming the entry assembly as the one it loads from, and never compiles the chain at run time | `CodeGenerationTests.A_host_outside_development_whose_handler_chain_has_no_generated_type_does_not_start` | A Host on the EF Core overload in Production, with a public Wolverine handler added through the new `configure` parameter and its own database. Red once without the Kit's `TypeLoadMode.Static`: the Host started, compiling the chain (no exception thrown). Red once without the Kit's `ApplicationAssembly`: "could not be loaded from the configured ApplicationAssembly 'PouryaKit.Messaging.Wolverine'". |
| A Kit Host starts outside Development, as a real process, from a build without the runtime compiler, with no generated code | `RabbitMqDeliveryTests.A_product_catalog_creates_is_applied_by_inventory_host_running_as_its_own_process_with_the_operation_context` | The child process runs `Inventory.Host` in Production now (it ran Development), in the tests' configuration: Release under the package validation, where `Inventory.Host` has no `WolverineFx.RuntimeCompilation`, so Development could not start it. Static with no registry: Wolverine logs its fallback warning and starts; the Kit's deliveries need no generated code. Its readiness wait is the broker queue's consumer, not `/alive`. `SharedStoreSchemaTests` starts its Host in Production in the test process the same way. |
| Every test Host runs Development from any build | The full `ModularMonolith.Tests` suite in Debug (`verify`) and in Release (the package validation's runtime suite) | `PouryaKit.Testing` references `WolverineFx.RuntimeCompilation`, as ADR 0004 allows; `PackageGraphTests` allows it there and nowhere else. |
| `codegen write` writes the Host's generated code, and a Release publish holds the Host built with it and neither `Wolverine.RuntimeCompilation.dll` nor `JasperFx.RuntimeCompiler.dll`, for each Host: both generated Hosts and the Proving Sample's two | `scripts/packaging/validate.py`, `production_publish` and `check_production_publish`, on `template-monolith`, `template-modular-monolith` and, since the fix round (review finding F1 of `cbdfc56`), the `modular-monolith` consumer's copies of `ModularMonolith.Host` and `Inventory.Host`; `ProductionPublishTests` in `scripts/packaging/test_validation.py` | `dotnet run --project src/Acme.Shop.Host -c Release --no-build --no-launch-profile -- codegen write` (it compiles nothing, so the Release build serves), then `dotnet restore -p:Configuration=Release` and `dotnet publish -c Release --no-restore`. A Debug-evaluated restore lists the compiler, and a Release build with `--no-restore` after it carried both dlls (observed on `Inventory.Host`), hence the Release restore. The unit test's negative cases: each compiler dll present, a Host built without the generated code, no `codegen write` output; red once with the compiler check removed. The sample Hosts' step runs on the consumer's copy of the sample, so the checkout gets no `Internal/Generated`. Red once for them on a copy of the first run's sample consumer: with `Inventory.Host`'s reference made unconditional, its publish "carries the runtime compiler: Wolverine.RuntimeCompilation.dll, JasperFx.RuntimeCompiler.dll"; unchanged, both Hosts passed. |
| The generated smoke tests host `Program` through the JasperFx command line | The generated `HostTests`, in both templates, under `validate.py` | `JasperFxEnvironment.AutoStartHost`, as Wolverine's integration testing guide requires with its command line; a probe of `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` over the sample Host without it failed ("Unknown argument or flag" for the factory's settings, the Host never started) and passed with it. The test project references `WolverineFx.RuntimeCompilation` itself. *Superseded on 2026-10-10 by ticket #87: the smoke tests compose the Host through `AppHost`, never through `Program`, so nothing is set for JasperFx, and the test project gets `WolverineFx.RuntimeCompilation` through `PouryaKit.Testing`.* |
| Wolverine's message storage is still built when the Host starts | The Production starts in this table, each on an empty database | The guide's sample sets `Production.ResourceAutoCreate = AutoCreate.None`; the Kit does not. |
| `check-env` in Production fails on a missing handler registry, which the start only warns about | By hand: the sample Host's Release build, `dotnet ModularMonolith.Host.dll check-env --file …` in Production | `All pre build types exist for Wolverine.Runtime.Handlers.HandlerGraph` failed with `MissingTypeException` for `HandlerRegistryCodeFile` in `ModularMonolith.Host`, from the profile's `AssertAllPreGeneratedTypesExist`. |
| Each generated Host, published in Release, starts as a real process in Production with the static mode against PostgreSQL and answers from the database | By hand on `validate.py`'s two publishes (commit `cbdfc56`): `postgres:18.6` in a container, `dotnet tool restore` and `dotnet ef database update --project src/Modules/<Module>/<Module> --startup-project src/Acme.Shop.Host --context <Module>DbContext` for each Module in the generated solution, then `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Production` `dotnet Acme.Shop.Host.dll` in the publish folder with `ConnectionStrings__app` and `Authentication__Schemes__Bearer__*` set | Both, started within 1 s: "The Wolverine code generation mode is Static with pre-generated types being loaded from Acme.Shop.Host", "Using pre-generated Wolverine HandlerRegistry (0 handler types); skipping assembly scan", Wolverine's message storage created on the empty database, and `GET /catalog/v2/products?size=1` 200 with an empty Page from PostgreSQL. `/alive` answered 401: the service defaults map it in Development only (#52), so the ticket's "answers `/alive`" does not apply in Production (corrected on the ticket). *Superseded on 2026-10-08 by ticket #64: the service defaults map `/alive` in every environment, and both published Hosts answer it 200 in Production (the #64 section below).* The publish folders hold no `Wolverine.RuntimeCompilation.dll` or `JasperFx.RuntimeCompiler.dll`; `Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.dll` is there through `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design`. |
| `codegen preview` works, and `codegen write` and `codegen preview` need no runtime compiler | The same publishes: `dotnet Acme.Shop.Host.dll codegen preview` in Production; the sample Host: `dotnet run -- codegen write` | `preview` exited 0 from the compiler-free publish of both and printed the registry. Of JasperFx 2.80.2's `GenerateCodeCommand` actions, only `test` resolves `IAssemblyGenerator`. |
| Packages | `WolverineFx.RuntimeCompilation` 6.46.0 (`MIT`), now referenced by `PouryaKit.Testing` and the generated test projects, by the Hosts in Debug builds only | Recorded in [`docs/provenance/PouryaKit.Testing.md`](provenance/PouryaKit.Testing.md) and [`ModularMonolith.md`](provenance/ModularMonolith.md); the Testing package's closure is 110 packages. No other Kit package changed its references. |
| Out of scope | | A Dockerfile or CI publish pipeline for the Kit's own Hosts; generated code committed in the Kit repository (`.gitignore` covers the sample Hosts' `Internal/Generated`); any change to message storage creation; a second mode switch. |

## Wave 2 template names as typed, ticket #59

Spec #33 and the owner's decision of 2026-10-07 that a name a namespace cannot hold is fixed, not documented (#53's fix round had documented it). The reference is Aspire's `aspire-starter` 13.6.1 (`microsoft/aspire` commit `3751e615`, `Aspire.ProjectTemplates`): generated with `-n acme-shop`, it names the folder `acme-shop.ApiService`, references `..\acme-shop.ApiService\acme-shop.ApiService.csproj` and writes `namespace acme_shop.Web`, because its source name `Aspire-StarterApplication.1` has a form as typed and a namespace-safe form that differ. The Kit keeps its `sourceName` and gets the same outcome with a `derived` symbol (dotnet templating wiki, "Reference for template.json").

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A generated solution restores and builds for `-n acme-shop`, `Acme.Shop`, `acme_shop` and `My Shop`: folders, files and the paths the solution and project references name carry the name as typed, the code its namespace-safe form, and the broker queue the derived service name | The four-name probe below, both templates from the produced package; `validate.py`, `template-monolith` generated with `-n Acme.Shop` and `template-modular-monolith` with `-n acme-shop`, each restored, built, smoke-tested and published | Before the fix, on a pack of `7bce996`'s templates, `-n acme-shop` generated `src/acme-shop.Host/` while the solution named `src/acme_shop.Host/acme_shop.Host.csproj`, and `dotnet restore` failed with `MSB3202` ("The project file … `acme_shop.Host.csproj` was not found") for the Host, the service defaults and the tests. |
| The template's own solution and project files name each project's path through the token `PouryaKitAppName` of the `projectName` symbol (`derived`, `valueSource` `name`, the `identity` form), never through the `sourceName`, and name only projects the template generates; the symbol is the same in both templates, and its token holds no form of the `sourceName` | `TemplateTests.Each_template_names_its_projects_paths_through_the_name_as_typed_the_same_way_in_both` | Negative cases: the old `src/ModularMonolith.Host/ModularMonolith.Host.csproj` line in the solution, and a project reference through the token to a project the template does not generate. Red once with the monolith solution's Host line put back to the old one. The packed sample files keep `ModularMonolith` for their namespaces. |
| The template's restore post-action restores the solution under its new name | The four-name probe: each name generated without `--no-restore` | The `primaryOutputs` path `ModularMonolith.slnx` is renamed with the files, so `-n acme-shop` restores `acme-shop.slnx`. |
| Out of scope | | The `sourceName` and the Proving Sample, unchanged; a `-n` validation (the engine has none). The generated project files set no `RootNamespace`, so MSBuild's default, the project's name with spaces as underscores (`acme-shop.Tests`), stays; all four names build with it. |

`python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the ticket's source (commit `d2ea803`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.90`, with the Release runtime suite at 723 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. The modular monolith, generated as `acme-shop.slnx` with `src/acme-shop.Host` and `tests/acme-shop.Tests`, restored, built, passed its smoke tests and published `acme-shop.Host.dll` in Release with the generated code (55 s); the monolith took 50 s of the run's 512 s.

The four-name probe ran on that run's produced packages: `PouryaKit.Templates.0.1.0-preview.0.90` installed into a hive of its own, each template generated with each name without `--no-restore`, under a folder whose `NuGet.Config` took `PouryaKit.*` from the produced packages only, with its own package cache, then `dotnet build` on the solution. All eight solutions restored through the template's post-action and built with 0 warnings and 0 errors:

| `-n` | Solution and Host project, in both templates | Namespace in code | Derived service name |
|---|---|---|---|
| `acme-shop` | `acme-shop.slnx`, `src/acme-shop.Host/acme-shop.Host.csproj` | `acme_shop.Host`, `acme_shop.Tests` | `acme-shop` |
| `Acme.Shop` | `Acme.Shop.slnx`, `src/Acme.Shop.Host/Acme.Shop.Host.csproj` | `Acme.Shop.Host`, `Acme.Shop.Tests` | `acme-shop` |
| `acme_shop` | `acme_shop.slnx`, `src/acme_shop.Host/acme_shop.Host.csproj` | `acme_shop.Host`, `acme_shop.Tests` | `acme-shop` |
| `My Shop` | `My Shop.slnx`, `src/My Shop.Host/My Shop.Host.csproj` | `My_Shop.Host`, `My_Shop.Tests` | `my-shop` |

## Wave 2 strict wire reading, reserved broker names and messaging terms, ticket #60

Spec #33 and the owner's decisions of 2026-10-07 on RabbitMQ naming and routing: the verified topology stays, wire payloads are read strictly (option (a)), unknown members are decided with evidence, the `amq.` prefix is refused, service-name uniqueness is a deployment rule, and three messaging terms join the Glossary. The reference baseline is .NET 10 System.Text.Json's own options (`RespectRequiredConstructorParameters`, `RespectNullableAnnotations`, `JsonUnmappedMemberHandling`) through Wolverine 6.46.0's `UseSystemTextJsonForSerialization`; ADR 0006's #60 amendment records the decisions, the decompiled dead-letter path and the trade-off.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A received envelope whose body lacks a member its contract requires, or carries null for a non-nullable one, is never handled: it is dead-lettered at the receiving Host's broker queue with the `JsonException` naming the member, and no subscription receives a copy | `StrictWireReadingTests.A_body_without_a_member_its_contract_requires_or_with_null_for_one_is_dead_lettered_at_the_host_queue_with_the_error_and_never_handled`, two cases, two Hosts on RabbitMQ 4.3.6 and PostgreSQL 18.6: the Catalog Host publishes a contract of another shape under the wire name `Inventory.Host` receives | Red once with Wolverine reading through `UsePouryaKitJson`: both cases ran the handler with a null name. |
| A member the body carries and the receiving contract lacks is skipped, and the handler gets the members it has (the decision: skip, not `Disallow`) | `StrictWireReadingTests.A_body_with_a_member_its_contract_lacks_is_handled_with_the_members_it_has` | Red once with `JsonUnmappedMemberHandling.Disallow`: the envelope was dead-lettered and never applied. |
| HTTP bodies keep the lenient reading: a member a request body lacks binds as its default and the validator answers it | `WebTests.A_body_without_a_member_binds_it_as_its_default_and_validation_answers_400_for_it`; `PouryaKit.ArchitectureTests` `SharedJsonTests.Only_messaging_wolverine_reads_strictly_and_web_cqrs_and_caching_keep_the_lenient_reading` | Red once with Web applying `UsePouryaKitWireJson`: binding refused the body, a 400 without the validator's errors, and the structure test listed Web as a strict reader. No earlier Web test sent a body the strict reading would refuse. |
| A wire name, subscription name or service name beginning with `amq.`, in any case, is refused at registration with the rule in the message: `Publish`, `Receive`, `Subscribe` and `UsePouryaKitRabbitMq` | `IntegrationEventWiringTests.A_composition_root_fails_on` (three cases); `IntegrationEventWiringTests.A_service_name_rabbitmq_reserves_is_refused_when_the_host_joins_the_broker` | Red once with the prefix allowed (four failed) and with a case-sensitive check (the capitals case failed). A probe against RabbitMQ 4.3.6 refused the queue `amq.inventory` and the exchange `amq.product-created.v1` with 403 `ACCESS_REFUSED` ("contains reserved prefix 'amq.*'") and declared `AMQ.inventory`; the Kit refuses every case because it compares names ignoring case. No sample, template or test name begins with it. |
| Writing a null into a non-nullable member under the strict options fails with `JsonException` | `PouryaKit.Domain.Tests` `WireJsonTests.The_wire_options_refuse_to_write_null_into_a_non_nullable_member` (review finding F1) | Red once with `RespectNullableAnnotations` off: the null was written. That the failure lands in the Unit of Work's commit, where Wolverine's EF Core outbox serializes the envelope, is read from the decompiled outbox, not tested. |
| Terms | `GLOSSARY.md`: Wire name, Subscription, Service name; the Kit skill's terms line | |
| Out of scope | | The verified topology, the `serviceName` parameter, a contract-identity header and a schema registry (option (b), not recommended). Service-name uniqueness has no check: a replica and a foreign Host are indistinguishable on the broker. |

`python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the ticket's source (commit `9b5922a`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.91`, with the Release runtime suite at 732 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. A first run on the same commit failed one runtime test, `CachingTests.With_redis_a_second_host_reads_the_entry_the_first_stored_without_running_its_handler`, whose own connection to its Redis container was refused (`RedisConnectionException` in `WaitUntilStoredAsync`); the rerun passed it unchanged, and it passed in both full Debug runs of the ticket.

On the merge of `feat/wave-2` at `29cf516` (#61) into the ticket's branch, `python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the merge commit (`6528359`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.93`, with the Release runtime suite at 742 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. The merged tree's full suites passed first: ModularMonolith.Tests 484 passed, ArchitectureTests 96 passed, `RabbitMqDeliveryTests` 19 passed.

## Wave 2 Module seam and composition checks, ticket #61

Spec #33, the owner's decisions of 2026-10-07 (A: a Module's Features live in its definition's assembly, inferred, with
named assemblies as the override, [ADR 0001](adr/0001-one-host-container.md)'s #61 amendment; B: a cacheable query is
never silent, [ADR 0002](adr/0002-no-mediator-dependency.md)'s #61 amendment) and the design-stage Codex review of the
Kit API cleanup, whose six findings the ticket's brief carries. The plan's own sketch is the reference: section D's
`module.Publish<ProductRenamedV1>("…")` with no Module argument ([ADR 0006](adr/0006-independent-delivery-through-a-routed-queue-per-subscription.md)'s #61 amendment).

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A publication, Snapshot, subscription, Notification Handler, delayed or recurring command declared without its Module in a Host of Modules fails the Host when it is built, naming the declaration, its form on the Module's builder and its form that names the Module | `CompositionCheckTests.A_module_declaration_made_without_its_module_in_a_host_of_modules_fails_the_host_when_built`, one case per declaration whose Module parameter #61 removed | Each declared on `builder.Services` of the Proving Sample's Host with the Stub Module; red once each with the check switched off: the Host then built. Before #61 such a declaration failed only at its first use, in the publisher, the Snapshot step, the delivery's Unit of Work router or the delayed-command scheduler (the removed Snapshot test named in this row proved the Snapshot case at commit). The Notification Handler is in a fixture assembly of its own, `ModularMonolith.Tests.Unowned`, since every handler of the Stub's assembly belongs to the Stub. Replaces `SnapshotTests.A_snapshot_declared_without_its_module_in_a_host_of_modules_fails_the_operation_that_would_publish_it`. |
| Every Module the Host adds counts as composed, so a declaration without its Module fails the build even when no Composition Root makes a Kit registration; a Host-level declaration naming a Module the Host never added fails the build too | `CompositionCheckTests.A_declaration_without_its_module_fails_the_build_of_a_host_whose_modules_make_no_kit_registration`; `CompositionCheckTests.A_declaration_for_a_module_the_host_never_composed_fails_the_host_when_built` | Review finding F4. Red once with `ModulesBuilder.Add`'s recording removed, and with the never-composed rule switched off. |
| A Composition Root that names another Module is refused while the Host is composed | `CompositionCheckTests.A_composition_root_that_names_another_module_is_refused_while_the_host_is_composed` | Red once with the refusal removed. |
| A `ConfigureContainer` after the Kit's entry stops the Host at start, naming the call, what was lost and the fix | `CompositionCheckTests.A_container_that_a_later_configure_container_replaced_stops_the_host_at_start_naming_the_call` | The only check left at start: the replaced factory ran neither the validation nor the composition checks. Red once with the runner's sentinel switched off. |
| A cacheable query in a Host without caching fails the Host when it is built, naming the queries and `AddPouryaKitCaching()` | `CompositionCheckTests.A_cacheable_query_in_a_host_without_caching_fails_the_host_when_built_naming_the_call` | Red once with the check switched off. The Proving Sample's Host and both templates call `AddPouryaKitCaching()` and build; the plain-services test compositions that hold the Stub's cacheable queries call it too. |
| A command of the Host's Features in a Host without a Unit of Work fails the Host when it is built; the Kit's own delivery commands do not count, so a Host with Wolverine, no declaration and no Unit of Work builds and starts | `CompositionCheckTests.A_features_command_in_a_host_without_a_unit_of_work_fails_the_host_when_built`; `CompositionCheckTests.A_host_with_wolverine_and_no_declaration_or_unit_of_work_builds_and_starts` | Hosts without Modules; the first red once with the check switched off, the second (review finding F3) with every registered command counted. A Module's own Unit of Work is still checked while the Host is composed (`ModuleWiringTests`). |
| A scanned handler of a command from an assembly no registration scans, as a Contracts assembly's, counts as a command of the Host's Features: in a Host without a Unit of Work it fails the Host when it is built | `CompositionCheckTests.A_scanned_handler_of_a_command_from_another_assembly_in_a_host_without_a_unit_of_work_fails_the_host_when_built` | Second review, finding F7: the check counted only a command whose own type was in a scanned assembly. The handler is `ModularMonolith.Tests.Misnamed`'s `SeparateCommandHandler`, the command `Unowned`'s `UnownedCommand`. Red once with the handler's assembly not counted: the Host built. |
| In a Host without Modules that installs the composition checks, a declaration naming a Module fails the build, and its line offers the application's form without a Module | `CompositionCheckTests.A_declaration_naming_a_module_in_a_host_without_modules_fails_the_host_when_built_offering_the_form_without_one` | Second review, finding F9. The refusal at commit of a Snapshot so declared remains only as the backstop of a Host without the checks; #62 removed its test, `SnapshotTests.A_snapshot_declared_with_a_module_in_an_application_without_modules_fails_the_operation_that_would_publish_it`, since `UsePouryaKitWolverine` now installs them. Red once with the line's advice the same as in a Host of Modules ("name a Module the Host composes"). |
| The declared-authorizer check keeps its moment, the container build, now on the composition checks | `SecurityTests.A_command_or_query_that_declares_a_permission_whose_check_no_composition_root_registered_fails_the_host_when_built`; the generated `HostTests.The_host_does_not_build_when_a_declared_permission_has_no_registered_check`, in both | Same message and moment as before #61 (review finding F1: a built, unstarted Host runs Gateway operations, so the checks run before `Build()` returns); red once with the checks run only at start. |
| A Module whose handler is registered by hand, with no `AddPouryaKitCqrs` anywhere in its Host, commits a command through its Gateway | `CompositionCheckTests.A_module_that_registers_its_handler_by_hand_commits_a_command_through_its_gateway` | Review finding F2: the Domain Event drain comes with the dispatcher's registration. Red once with the drain registered only by `AddPouryaKitCqrs`. |
| A Module's builder supplies its Module and its Features' assembly; the Host records each Module with its Feature assemblies for the checks of #62 | `CompositionCheckTests.The_host_composition_names_each_module_with_the_feature_assembly_its_builder_inferred`; the whole suite, whose Composition Roots name neither | Red once with the inference removed (every Composition Root's registrations then scan nothing) and with the Module record removed. |
| One operation classification for Modularity, Cqrs and Security | `OperationTypes`; the suite's ownership, registration and permission tests | Refactor: the Gateway suite stayed at 466 passed before the seam. |
| Packages | No package added; `PouryaKit.Cqrs` references `Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection` and `Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting.Abstractions` 10.0.12 (both `MIT`, already in every Host), `PouryaKit.Modularity` drops `Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection` | `PackageGraphTests` and [plan section C](foundation-review.md#allowed-references). |
| Out of scope | | Ticket #62: the two start checks of their own shape (Messaging.Wolverine's `StartupCheck`, Web.OpenApi's `UnmappedApiVersionsCheck`) move onto the contract there, with its own start-time checks, the limiter order, resilience stacking, `RequireIdempotencyKey` on GET, connection strings at start, the tenant claim and the endpoint and Notification Handler completeness checks. A forgotten validator registration stays undetectable without a reverse reference. |

`python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the ticket's source (commit `5944f3f`, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.94`, with the Release runtime suite at 733 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. The clean consumers exercise the changed API from the produced packages: `single-module` calls `AddPouryaKitCompositionChecks`, `PouryaKit.Cqrs` names the seam and the check contract, `PouryaKit.Messaging.Wolverine` declares a delayed command for a named Module from the Host's services, `PouryaKit.Web` declares endpoints by assembly, `PouryaKit.Caching` reads the cache marker; the generated smoke tests proved that a declared permission without its check stopped each generated Host at start, as the checks ran at `5944f3f`; the fix round below moved them to the container build.

After the review's fix round (findings F1 to F6 of `0ffa866`: the composition checks run when the container is built, the dispatcher's registration brings the Domain Event drain, the Unit of Work check counts the commands of the Host's Features, every added Module counts as composed and a Module never composed is refused, two doc fixes), merged onto `a87e637`, `python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on commit `974dd2c` (before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.104`, with the Release runtime suite at 747 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped. The generated smoke tests prove again that a declared permission without its check stops each generated Host when it is built.

After the second review's findings F7 to F9 of `7d2366f` (the Unit of Work check counts a scanned handler of a command from another assembly; the ownership check's line offers the application's form in a Host without Modules; the skill, ADR 0006 and the Snapshot docs say the checks run at the build and the commit-time Snapshot refusal is a backstop), merged onto `b318192` (#62), `python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on commit `bbd3b96` (before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.100`, with the Release runtime suite at 755 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped.

## Wave 2 a misconfigured Host fails clearly, never silently, ticket #62

Spec #33 and the API-misuse review of 2026-10-07 with its design-stage Codex dispositions (API-01, API-02, API-04, API-05):
a wrong composition fails at composition where the builder is in hand, when the Host is built or starts otherwise, or
where its mistake is built, with a message naming the fix, and never runs degraded without a word. The checks run at two
moments with one report shape, by the orchestrator's decision of 2026-10-08: composition checks (`ICompositionCheck`)
when the container is built, as #61's fix round runs them, and startup checks (`IStartupCheck`) at `StartingAsync` for
what only the built application shows; Wolverine's queue durability keeps Wolverine's own moment, `StartedAsync`. The
reference baseline is ASP.NET Core 10's `AuthenticationMiddleware`, which sets `IAuthenticationFeature` on every request
it sees, `WebApplication`'s automatic `UseRouting`, `UseAuthentication` and `UseAuthorization`,
`Microsoft.Extensions.Http`'s handler builder filters, and #61's composition-check entry.

| Scenario | Proven by | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| A rate limiter that runs before authentication throws at the request, naming the order, instead of putting every caller in one partition | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_rate_limiter_placed_before_authentication_fails_the_request_instead_of_putting_every_caller_in_one_partition` | Red once with the check off: the request was served. The signal is `IAuthenticationFeature`, not the user's identity or endpoint metadata. `WebApplication` adds authentication ahead of the application's middleware unless the Host calls it, so only an explicit `UseAuthentication()` after `UseRateLimiter()` reaches the throw. A limiter before `UseRouting()` is not detected (routing marks no request it matched nothing for); recorded in [web.md](../.agents/skills/pourya-kit/references/web.md). The anonymous and per-Actor partitions keep their behaviour (`WebDefaultsTests`). |
| A Host that registers no authentication is not refused for the order: its anonymous callers partition by the Actor each endpoint names | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_rate_limit_in_a_host_without_authentication_partitions_public_callers_by_the_actor_their_endpoint_names` | Review finding F6: the limiter throws only when authentication is registered (`IAuthenticationSchemeProvider`) and has not run. Red once with that condition removed: the request threw the order message. |
| A client whose completed handler chain holds two resilience handlers, as when it adds its own over the service defaults', throws naming `RemoveAllResilienceHandlers()` whenever it is created | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_client_that_adds_a_resilience_pipeline_over_the_service_defaults_one_fails_whenever_it_is_created` | Red once with the check off: the client was created with both pipelines. The replacement scenarios keep their behaviour (`HostRecipeTests`, `OutgoingHttpTests`). |
| `RequireIdempotencyKey()` on a Module's GET endpoint fails the Host's start | `MisconfiguredHostTests.An_endpoint_that_requires_an_idempotency_key_on_a_get_fails_the_host_at_start` | Red once with the refusal off: the Host started. Whether the command opts in stays a dispatch check ([ADR 0002](adr/0002-no-mediator-dependency.md)'s #62 amendment). An endpoint mapped for no method was not refused here; since the final structural review it is ([below](#package-a-web-security-and-two-doc-lines)). |
| A connection string `UsePouryaKitPostgres` or `AddPouryaKitDapper` names, missing or blank in the final configuration, fails the Host when it is built, naming the connection and its key; nothing connects | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_connection_string_the_configuration_lacks_or_leaves_blank_fails_the_host_when_built_naming_its_key` | Red once with each check off, Postgres's and Dapper's: its line was missing. Wolverine's and RabbitMQ's Host-builder APIs keep their checks at composition. |
| The tenant claim is stated once: Web reads `PouryaKitClaimTypes.Tenant`, now in `PouryaKit.Cqrs`, by default | `JwtBearerTests.A_verified_token_reaches_the_operation_as_its_subject_in_its_tenant`; `JwtBearerTests.The_token_s_own_actor_and_tenant_claims_never_stand_in_for_the_configured_ones` | The Proving Sample's Host and both templates no longer set Web's `TenantClaim`. Red once with the default off: the tenant was null. |
| A Module whose Notification Handlers no registration declares fails the Host when it is built, naming `AddPouryaKitNotificationHandlers` | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_module_whose_notification_handlers_no_registration_declares_fails_the_host_when_built_naming_the_call` | Red once with the check off: the Host built. |
| A Module whose endpoints no registration declares stops the Host at start, naming `AddPouryaKitEndpoints` | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_module_whose_endpoints_no_registration_declares_stops_the_host_at_start_naming_the_call` | A startup check, since the application maps its routes after `Build()`; it runs whether or not the Module declares API versions. Red once with the check off: the Host started. |
| Endpoints declared but never mapped stop the Host at start, naming `MapPouryaKitModuleEndpoints` | `MisconfiguredHostTests.Endpoints_declared_but_never_mapped_stop_the_host_at_start_naming_the_call` | Red once with the check off: the Host started. |
| The two start checks of their own shape keep their behaviour on the one entry: the API versions check as a startup check, the Outbox check as a composition check | `OpenApiTests.A_declaration_for_a_route_group_no_module_maps_fails_the_host_at_start`; `NotificationTests.A_host_that_declares_notification_handlers_but_writes_no_outbox_fails_when_built`; `IntegrationEventWiringTests.Building_fails_when_modules_publish_integration_events_and_the_host_has_no_outbox` | Red once with each moved check unregistered. `UnmappedApiVersionsCheck` left `IStartupFilter`, and `StartupCheck`'s Outbox part became `OutboxCheck`; the rest, the queue and broker-listener durability checks, is `DurableQueuesCheck` at `StartedAsync`: Wolverine applies its endpoint policies and the Host's queue configuration in `Endpoint.Compile`, when it starts, so an earlier check would read every queue's default (`NotificationTests.A_host_whose_subscription_queue_does_not_store_its_deliveries_fails_at_start`, `RabbitMqDeliveryTests`). The Outbox tests now fail when the Host is built. |
| The startup checks run at `StartingAsync`, after the replaced-container check, in one exception | `MisconfiguredHostTests.A_module_whose_endpoints_no_registration_declares_stops_the_host_at_start_naming_the_call`; `MisconfiguredHostTests.Endpoints_declared_but_never_mapped_stop_the_host_at_start_naming_the_call`; `OpenApiTests.A_declaration_for_a_route_group_no_module_maps_fails_the_host_at_start` | Red once with the runner's startup checks off: all three Hosts started. |
| A Kit Host that never installs the checks fails when it starts, naming `AddPouryaKitCompositionChecks()` | `OpenApiTests.A_web_host_that_never_installs_the_kits_checks_fails_at_start_naming_the_entry` | Review finding F2: the runner comes with the dispatcher's registration, which every Kit Host reaches, and an install marker tells a Host that never installed the checks from one whose container a later `ConfigureContainer` replaced. Red once with the runner's registration removed from the dispatcher's: the Host started unverified, and so did `OpenApiTests.A_declaration_for_a_route_group_no_module_maps_fails_the_host_at_start`'s. |
| A Wolverine Host without Modules installs the checks itself: `UsePouryaKitWolverine` calls `AddPouryaKitCompositionChecks()` | `NotificationTests.A_host_that_declares_notification_handlers_but_writes_no_outbox_fails_when_built`; `IntegrationEventWiringTests.Building_fails_when_modules_publish_integration_events_and_the_host_has_no_outbox` | Neither test Host calls the entry. Plain-services test Hosts with Wolverine now build a validated container: the Stub's whole assembly cannot be registered there, so two register the one command they send by hand (`NotificationTests.An_application_without_modules_delivers_its_notifications_with_their_receipts`, `SharedStoreSchemaTests`); three Wolverine-refusal tests register a stand-in Outbox, and `CodeGenerationTests` a Unit of Work. `SnapshotTests.A_snapshot_declared_with_a_module_in_an_application_without_modules_fails_the_operation_that_would_publish_it` is removed: such a Host now fails when it is built (#61's ownership check, `CompositionCheckTests.A_declaration_for_a_module_the_host_never_composed_fails_the_host_when_built`), so its commit-time refusal stays only as a backstop. |
| Out of scope | | A forgotten `AddPouryaKitValidation` (no Host entry; a check from elsewhere would need a reverse reference); `Receive<T>` with no `Subscribe`, documented. |

`python3 scripts/packaging/validate.py --no-fetch` on the ticket's source (commit `bf83fe3`, the merge of `feat/wave-2` at `a87e637` plus #62, before this paragraph was added) printed `PASS 21 packages, 24 clean consumers (2 generated from templates, smoke tests passed: template-monolith (-n Acme.Shop) 4, template-modular-monolith (-n acme-shop) 5), negative controls and MinVer proof` at version `0.1.0-preview.0.99`, with the Release runtime suite at 749 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped.
