## Web conventions for other transports, ticket #119

Ticket #119 of spec #115, the Web prefactor gRPC (#134) builds on: two public members, nothing else changes. `BeginOperationPerRequest()` on a `RouteGroupBuilder` is the begin-the-Operation convention `MapPouryaKitModuleEndpoints` applies to each Module's group; `OperationContextHttpHeaders`, Web's one codec, is public with one public member, `Pairs(context)`, which the outgoing handler writes. Environment: macOS (Darwin 27.0.0, arm64), .NET SDK 10.0.402, Docker 29.8.2, Archify 3.0.1 with Playwright's Chromium headless.

| Criterion | Test or change | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A route group outside a Module's group applies the convention and its endpoints inject the Operation Context as Module endpoints do | `WebConventionTests.A_root_group_that_begins_the_operation_gives_its_endpoints_the_context_a_module_endpoint_gets`: a Host maps the Stub's `ReadOperationContextEndpoint` on a root group `/outside` with `RequireAuthorization().BeginOperationPerRequest()`; a trusted peer's headers continue Ada's Operation in tenant `acme` with the peer's correlation and causation, a person runs as itself with its tenant claim in a new chain, and an anonymous caller of the `AllowAnonymousAs` path runs as its named Actor | Red once with the convention not applied: `Expected: OK, Actual: InternalServerError`, the endpoint's binding threw `No Operation Context was set for this scope` |
| The public reader returns the header names and validated values the outgoing handler writes | `WebConventionTests.The_public_header_pairs_are_the_ones_the_outgoing_handler_writes_in_messagings_names`: a client with `AddPouryaKitOperationContext()` sends a request whose Kit headers equal `Pairs(context)`, in Messaging's `OperationContextHeaders` names and order; a tenant outside the Kit's rule is refused with `InvalidOperationException` before any pair is returned | Red once with the causation header renamed in the codec: `Assert.Equal() Failure`, `pouryakit-causation` for `pouryakit-causation-id` |
| The internal members otherwise stay internal | Public surface delta of `PouryaKit.Web`: `WebEndpointRouteBuilderExtensions.BeginOperationPerRequest(RouteGroupBuilder)`, the class `OperationContextHttpHeaders` and its `Pairs(OperationContext)`. `Write`, `Continued`, `Rule` and `IsHeaderValue` became or stay internal; `HttpCallerContext`, `AnonymousActor` and `OperationContextHttpHandler` are unchanged. The Kit has no public API baseline test | `dotnet build PouryaKit.slnx`: 0 warnings, 0 errors (CS1591 on) |
| The architecture document and the Web reference name the two members | [`docs/architecture/README.md`](architecture/README.md): the Web table, the request's Operation, continuation, options and outgoing HTTP bullets; the skill's [web.md](../.agents/skills/pourya-kit/references/web.md) and its `SKILL.md` row; a dated amendment of [ADR 0007](adr/0007-operation-context-set-by-the-scope-opener.md). ## Email, ticket #124

Ticket #124 of spec #115: the email contract `PouryaKit.Email` (`IEmailSender.SendAsync(EmailMessage, CancellationToken)` returning a `Result`, `EmailErrors.InvalidAddress`), on Domain alone, and the MailKit sender `PouryaKit.Email.MailKit` (`AddPouryaKitMailKit()`, `PouryaKitMailKitOptions`), a singleton built in its registration's factory from options the Host binds from `PouryaKit:Email` and the Kit validates at start, sending on a connection per send from one registration, the choice spec #115 left to the ticket. `ModularMonolith.Host` composes it; the Stub's Notification Handlers (`tests/ModularMonolith.Tests/Stub/StubEmail.cs`) send. `EmailTests` runs the application on a database of its own with its mail sent to a Papercut container (`changemakerstudiosus/papercut-smtp:7.8.0` through `Testcontainers.Papercut` 4.15.0) and reads what arrived from Papercut's HTTP API. Environment: macOS (Darwin 27.0.0, arm64), .NET SDK 10.0.402, Docker 29.8.2, MailKit 4.18.1.

| Criterion | Test or change | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A Notification Handler sends through a Papercut container and the mail arrives | `EmailTests.A_notification_handler_sends_a_mail_that_arrives_once_its_operation_commits`: a committed note raises `NoteMailedDomainEvent`; its Notification Handler sends, and one mail with the note's subject arrives, to the recipient, from the configured sender, with the body | Red once with the sender's `SmtpClient.SendAsync` call removed: `0 of 1 mails with the subject … arrived in 30 s.` |
| A rolled-back business Operation sends nothing (G.2) | `EmailTests.A_rolled_back_operation_sends_no_mail`: a note that raises the mail event and then fails is rolled back with `Note.RefusedAfterRaising`; the mail of a committed note sent after it arrives, and none with the rolled-back note's subject does | Red once with `EfUnitOfWork.RunAsync` committing whatever the command returned: `Assert.Empty() Failure: Collection was not empty` |
| A send that succeeds followed by a delivery failure is retried and the mail arrives twice, the stated limit | `EmailTests.A_mail_whose_delivery_fails_after_sending_it_is_sent_again_by_the_retry`: the handler sends, then fails its first delivery; the retry sends again and succeeds: its Receipt is committed, so the delivery is over, and exactly two mails with the note's subject arrived (review F2). The skill's [email.md](../.agents/skills/pourya-kit/references/email.md#limits) states the limit: no transactional mail (spec #115, "Out of Scope") | Red once with the handler never failing after its send: `1 of 2 mails with the subject … arrived in 30 s.`, and in the fix round `Assert.Equal() Failure: Values differ`. Red once with the handler failing after every send: `The retried delivery committed no Receipt.` |
| Options are bound from configuration and validated at start | `ModularMonolith.Host` binds `PouryaKitMailKitOptions` from `PouryaKit:Email`. `EmailTests.A_host_whose_email_options_name_no_smtp_server_fails_to_start_naming_the_option`: with `--PouryaKit:Email:Host=` the Host's start throws `OptionsValidationException` whose one failure is `PouryaKitMailKitOptions.Host names no SMTP server; …`. `EmailTests.A_recipient_that_is_not_a_mailbox_address_fails_the_send_with_its_error_and_sends_nothing`: `ada`, which MimeKit reads as a mailbox without a domain, fails with `EmailErrors.InvalidAddress` before any connection. `EmailTests.A_host_whose_email_security_is_not_a_secure_socket_option_fails_to_start_naming_the_option` (review F1): `--PouryaKit:Email:Security=99` binds, and MailKit would connect in plain SMTP for it; the start fails with `PouryaKitMailKitOptions.Security is not a SecureSocketOptions value; …` | Red once with the host check always passing: `Assert.Throws() Failure: No exception was thrown`. Red once with the `Security` check always passing: `Assert.Throws() Failure: No exception was thrown`. Red once with the domain check removed: Papercut refused the recipient, `MailKit.Net.Smtp.SmtpCommandException` |
| The package graph and licence rows pass | `PackageGraph`: `PouryaKit.Email` references Domain and no package (`NoPackages`), with a closure case in `PackageGraphTests`; `PouryaKit.Email.MailKit` references Email, `MailKit` 4.18.1, `Microsoft.Extensions.Options` 10.0.12 and `System.Security.Cryptography.Pkcs` 10.0.13, a security floor over the 10.0.0 MimeKit asks for (CVE-2026-84008, CVE-2026-70162; NuGet's audit fails the build without it), recorded in plan section C for the owner to confirm. Every package in both closures is `MIT` | `PackageGraphTests` and `LicenceTests` pass. ## Localization, ticket #125

Ticket #125 of spec #115: the optional `PouryaKit.Localization`, over the ASP.NET Core shared framework and `PouryaKit.Cqrs` alone. A Module calls `AddPouryaKitLocalization()` on its builder, which records its Feature assemblies through `IModuleServices.FeatureAssembliesOf` and registers the framework's `IStringLocalizer<T>`; the internal `HeaderRequestCulture` sets each request's culture from the `Accept-Language` header alone, English or Persian with English the default, through a startup filter. Environment: macOS (Darwin 27.0.0, arm64), .NET SDK 10.0.402, Docker 29.8.2, Archify 3.0.1 with Playwright's Chromium headless.

| Criterion | Test or change | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| The same key resolves in English and Persian over HTTP | The Stub Module registers the package; `GreetQueryHandler` reads `Greeting` through `IStringLocalizer<StubMessages>` from `StubMessages.resx` and `StubMessages.fa.resx`, behind `GET /stub/greeting` and the Stub Module's Gateway. `LocalizationTests.The_same_key_resolves_in_the_culture_of_the_accept_language_header`: no header, `en` and `de` answer the English value; `fa`, `fa-IR` and `de, fa;q=0.5` the Persian one | Red once with `UseRequestLocalization()` removed from the startup filter: the three Persian rows failed, the English ones passed |
| The Proving Sample's main Host runs without the package | Neither sample Host references it. `PackageGraphTests.The_sample_hosts_run_without_the_optional_localization` reads both Hosts' restored closures; `inventory.json`'s modular-monolith composition excludes `PouryaKit.Localization`, so the package validation refuses a packed sample that brings it | Red once with a project reference to the package added to `ModularMonolith.Host.csproj`: the test failed |
| The package adds no NuGet dependency; the skill shows a Module writing localized messages | `PackageGraph`'s row: `PouryaKit.Cqrs`, no package, the framework `Microsoft.AspNetCore.App`; `LicenceTests` reads its closure, Cqrs's. The skill's [localization.md](../.agents/skills/pourya-kit/references/localization.md) and its `SKILL.md` row; the plan's section C row and I.3 "Built" note; [provenance](provenance/PouryaKit.Localization.md); [`docs/architecture/README.md`](architecture/README.md#pouryakitlocalization) | `PackageGraphTests` and `LicenceTests` pass |

## File storage, ticket #127

Ticket #127 of spec #115: the file storage contract, `PouryaKit.Storage`, and its S3 provider, `PouryaKit.Storage.S3`. `IFileStorage` uploads, downloads and deletes a file under a key the caller chooses and signs a time-limited link, each call with a `CancellationToken` and returning a `Result`. `AddPouryaKitS3Storage()` registers it from `PouryaKitS3StorageOptions`, which the Host binds from configuration and which are validated when it starts. The proof is its own test project, `tests/PouryaKit.Storage.Tests`, with MinIO in Testcontainers: no criterion needs the Proving Sample to compose storage, and a provider test needs no PostgreSQL. Environment: macOS (Darwin 27.0.0, arm64), .NET SDK 10.0.402, Docker 29.8.2, `AWSSDK.S3` 4.0.104.4, `minio/minio:RELEASE.2025-04-22T22-12-26Z` (replaced by `rustfs/rustfs:1.0.2` on 2026-10-17, #148, after MinIO withdrew its images), Archify 3.0.1 with Playwright's Chromium headless.

| Criterion | Test or change | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Upload and download pass against a MinIO container | `S3StorageTests.An_uploaded_file_downloads_with_its_content_and_content_type`: a PDF uploaded through `IFileStorage`, resolved from a started Host whose options are bound from configuration, downloads with the same bytes and content type | |
| A missing object is the Kit's not-found Error, and delete passes | `S3StorageTests.A_deleted_file_downloads_as_the_not_found_error_and_deleting_it_again_succeeds`: after the delete, the download fails with exactly `StorageErrors.FileNotFound`, an `ErrorKind.NotFound` Error, and a second delete succeeds | Red once with the `NoSuchKeyException` mapping off: `Amazon.S3.Model.NoSuchKeyException : The specified key does not exist.` reached the caller |
| A presigned URL fetches the object | `S3StorageTests.A_presigned_url_reads_the_file_without_credentials_until_it_expires`: a plain `HttpClient` reads the file's bytes from a five-minute link, and a one-second link is refused with 403 after three seconds | Red once with the link always signed for HTTPS (the SDK's default): `HttpRequestException : The SSL connection could not be established` against MinIO's HTTP endpoint. ## Provider guarantees, ticket #147

Ticket #147 of spec #115, raised by three Codex threads on PR #143 after #124 and #127 closed: the one-provider guarantee of `PouryaKit.Storage.S3` and `PouryaKit.Email.MailKit` holds whatever the registration order, and an S3 presigned link refuses a lifetime S3 cannot sign. The ticket asked for a composition check and left its place open; the contract packages reference Domain only and the provider packages their contract and vendor SDK, while `ICompositionCheck` is Cqrs's, so each provider adds an options validation to the `ValidateOnStart()` it already had (the brief's shape): the internal `OneFileStorage` and `OneEmailSender` count the unkeyed registrations of the Host's final service collection when it starts, and name each implementation. They read the registrations rather than resolve the providers, since resolving would build each provider, whose factory reads the options being validated. The Host therefore fails when it starts, not when it is built. Environment: macOS (Darwin 27.0.0, arm64), .NET SDK 10.0.402, Docker 29.8.2, `AWSSDK.S3` 4.0.104.4, MailKit 4.18.1, `rustfs/rustfs:1.0.2`, Archify 3.0.1 with Playwright's Chromium headless.

| Criterion | Test or change | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| A Host with two `IFileStorage` registrations, in either order, fails naming both | `S3StorageTests.A_host_that_registers_another_file_storage_provider_fails_to_start_naming_both`, a theory over a second provider registered before and after `AddPouryaKitS3Storage`: `StartAsync` fails with an `OptionsValidationException` whose one failure names both implementations in registration order. `AddPouryaKitS3Storage` no longer throws at registration; a second call of its own fails the start the same way | Red once with the check off (`providers.Count >= 0`): both cases started, no `OptionsValidationException` thrown |
| The same for `IEmailSender` | `EmailTests.A_host_that_registers_another_email_sender_fails_to_start_naming_both`, the same theory over `AddPouryaKitMailKit`, on a Host built by hand with valid options, since the sample Host registers MailKit before any `configure` could add one first; `AddPouryaKitMailKit` registers with `AddSingleton`, no longer `TryAddSingleton`, which kept an earlier sender silently | Red once with the check off: both cases started |
| A presigned link over seven days returns a failed Result | `S3StorageTests.A_presigned_url_whose_lifetime_is_not_positive_or_over_seven_days_is_refused_with_its_error`: seven days and a second, zero and minus one second each fail with exactly `StorageErrors.LinkLifetimeOutOfRange`, an `ErrorKind.Validation` Error, before the SDK is called; a lifetime of zero or less threw `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` before, and the brief makes it the same failed Result. `S3StorageTests.A_presigned_url_of_seven_days_is_signed` holds the bound inclusive, and `A_presigned_url_reads_the_file_without_credentials_until_it_expires` stays green | Red once with the upper bound off: the seven-days-and-a-second case failed. Red once with the lower bound off: the zero and negative cases failed. Red once with seven days excluded (`>=`): the seven-day link failed |
